I mean **questions missing from the selection dropdown**, not necessarily missing from the passage itself.

Your screenshot shows **Question 1, Question 2, then Question 4**. Question 3 is skipped.

During the comparison, I checked **PrepTest 140, Section 4**:

- LSAT Lab’s dropdown lists all **27 questions**.
- LSAT U’s dropdown lists **25**, skipping **Questions 3 and 17**.
- Both questions exist in LSAT U’s underlying passage files.

The cause is an outdated generated lookup list. The dropdown reads that list, which wasn’t updated to include those questions.

So “restore missing questions” means **update the selector’s list so existing questions can be found and opened**. It does not mean inventing questions or changing answer keys.

Use the absolute paths above.\\r\\n\\r\\nProtocol is canonical in `~/ai-os/CLAUDE.md` (Session Start / Session End), run via two skills:\\r\\n\\r\\n- **`/pickup`** (start / \\"brief me\\"): reads `wiki/tasks.md` → `wiki/hot.md` → `wiki/dashboard.md` → `wiki/index.md` → latest `wiki/log.md` entry, then gives a 6-8 line briefing (Now / Next / open threads / suggested next) and waits.\\r\\n- **`/wrapup`** (handoff / \\"done for today\\"): proposes which tasks are done (confirm first), strikes them through in `wiki/tasks.md`, refreshes `wiki/hot.md`, appends a dated `wiki/log.md` entry, updates the task anchor, gives a 4-line confirmation.\\r\\n\\r\\n`wiki/tasks.md` is the living ledger of Now / Next / Done; finished work is struck through with a date so a new session never re-runs it.\\r\\n\\r\\nWhere LSATU truth lives in the vault (paths relative to `~/ai-os`):\\r\\n\\r\\nThe vault was rebuilt on 2026-07-16. **Live** pages:\\r\\n\\r\\n- Teaching voice (route ALL student-facing copy through it): `wiki/teaching-voice.md`\\r\\n- The tutoring business (offers, channels, priorities): `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`\\r\\n- Hubs: `wiki/tasks.md`, `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/log.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, `wiki/index.md`\\r\\n- A student\'s whole record (transcripts, notes, homework): `students/<name>/` — see `students/README.md` for the primary/mirror rule\\r\\n- LSAT teaching knowledge: `corpus/index.md` (fills by ingest)\\r\\n\\r\\n**Archived** — the pre-rebuild product knowledge is read-only reference at `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/`; it is not maintained and may be stale. Re-ingest a page through `raw/` if it becomes load-bearing again rather than editing it in place:\\r\\n\\r\\n- Product context: `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md` (flagship Main Conclusion bootcamp)\\r\\n- Design system (colors, type, role tokens): `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`\\r\\n- Code/source map: `…/synthesis/lsatu-source-map.md` (regenerable from this codebase)\\r\\n- Quality bar all bootcamps finish to: `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`\\r\\n- Concepts, entities, domain index: `…/concepts/`, `…/entities/`, `…/index.md`\\r\\n\\r\\nRules: `raw/` is immutable input, never edit it. Read knowledge from `wiki/`, not `raw/`. Use the `obsidian` CLI (`search`, `backlinks`, `read`) for graph traversal (needs the Obsidian app open); flat file reads work without it. When saving deliverables to the vault, use `[[wiki-link]]` syntax and `UPPERCASE-HYPHENATED.md` naming.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Commands\\r\\n\\r\\nScripts (from `package.json`):\\r\\n\\r\\n- `npm run dev` — Vite dev server on port 8080\\r\\n- `npm run build` — production build\\r\\n- `npm run build:dev` — build with development mode flags\\r\\n- `npm run preview` — serve the built bundle\\r\\n- `npm run lint` — ESLint flat config (`eslint.config.js`)\\r\\n- `npm run test` — Vitest single run (runner wired, no specs exist yet)\\r\\n- `npm run test:watch` — Vitest watch mode\\r\\n- Single test file: `npx vitest run path/to/file.test.ts`\\r\\n\\r\\nPackage manager: **npm**. `package-lock.json` is canonical. A stray `bun.lock` / `bun.lockb` exists in the repo — ignore it, do not switch tooling.\\r\\n\\r\\n- `npm run typecheck` — `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json`. Not optional before a push: `vite build` uses esbuild, which strips types without checking them, so a type error ships and auto-deploys. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on this.\\r\\n- `npm run test:e2e` / `npm run playwright` — Playwright e2e (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).\\r\\n\\r\\nNo Prettier. Formatting is ESLint-only.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Architecture (big picture)\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Routing** — React Router v6. Routes are registered in `src/App.tsx`, pages live in `src/pages/`. `/foyer` is the landing surface. Feature-gated pages are wrapped by `ProtectedRoute`.\\r\\n- **Auth + permissions** — Supabase Auth session plus Lovable Cloud Auth JWT fallback (`@lovable.dev/cloud-auth-js`). Per-feature access flags (`has_practice_access`, `has_drill_access`, `has_chat_access`, `is_admin`, ...) are resolved in `src/hooks/useUserPermissions.ts`. Session death is detected via JWT/401.\\r\\n- **Supabase integration** — client auto-generated at `src/integrations/supabase/client.ts` (Supabase JS v2). DB types are auto-generated — do not hand-edit. Edge Functions (Deno) in `supabase/functions/`: `admin-manage-users`, `analyze-stem-response`, `generate-micro-drill`, `motivation-engine`, `parse-drill-request`, `tutor-chat`, `voice-coach-respond`. SQL migrations in `supabase/migrations/`.\\r\\n- **State** — React Context for cross-cutting concerns (`AuthContext`, `UserSettingsContext`, `QuestionBankContext`, `ThemeContext`, `TimerContext`). TanStack Query v5 for server state.\\r\\n- **Primary navigation — Foyer** — `src/pages/AcademyFoyer.tsx` composes `src/components/foyer/FoyerSidebar.tsx` (state blocks + workspace nav) with `src/components/foyer/FoyerHeroRing.tsx` (3 nodes: Smart Drill, Resume, Ask Joshua). This is the primary nav for the product.\\r\\n- **Messaging / Inbox** — real messaging feature, not cosmetic. Lives in `src/components/inbox/` (`FloatingMessenger`, `ConversationView`, `ThreadList`) plus `src/hooks/useInbox.ts`. One ongoing 1:1 thread per student with the admin (Joshua). Supports PDF attachments. Gated by `has_chat_access`.\\r\\n- **Practice surfaces** — Drill and Wrong Answer Journal (WAJ) are real product surfaces, not launchers. Core logic in `src/lib/`: `adaptiveEngine`, `drillIntelligence`, `achievementEngine`, `gamification`, `questionLoader`, `streakSystem`, `wajService`.\\r\\n- **Admin** — `AdminDashboard` + `admin-manage-users` edge function. Must be desktop AND mobile responsive. Needs granular per-feature toggle control.\\r\\n- **UI primitives** — shadcn/ui (Radix + `class-variance-authority`) in `src/components/ui/`. Icons from `lucide-react` and `@tabler/icons-react`. Framer Motion for animation. Spline for 3D.\\r\\n- **Styling** — Tailwind 3.4 over HSL custom properties in `src/index.css`, surfaced as tokens in `tailwind.config.ts`. `DESIGN.md` is the visual authority; `.impeccable/design.json` is its machine-readable sidecar. Prefer tokens over ad-hoc hex values, and never add a token name whose CSS variable is not defined - three such names (`accent-bronze`, `accent-warm`, `glow-bronze`) shipped broken for five months and silently removed the focus ring from every shared Button.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Conventions\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Git workflow:** Always commit and push directly to `main`. Never create feature branches or pull requests unless explicitly asked. After completing any code change: stage relevant files, commit with a descriptive message, and push to `main`.\\r\\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session; a raw push can get rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges origin/main if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes.\\r\\n- **Never roll `main` back.** Do not use Lovable\'s \\"Revert to commit\\", \\"revert and resend\\", or history restore, and never reset or force-push `main`. On 2026-09-27 an accidental \\"revert and resend\\" (b998596) silently undid three merged PRs across 121 question-bank files, and students saw the wrong questions again. To undo your own work, `git revert <your SHA>` only. If a Lovable dialog offers a revert, dismiss it; if one fires, stop and tell Joshua before repairing anything.\\r\\n- Path alias `@/` → `./src/` (set in `vite.config.ts` and `tsconfig.json`).\\r\\n- TypeScript strict mode is **off** (`strictNullChecks: false`, `noImplicitAny: false`, `noUnusedLocals: false`). Do not flip these without explicit approval — it would cascade into a very large diff.\\r\\n- Lovable-specific plumbing that must not be removed:\\r\\n  - `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin injects `data-lov-id` in dev\\r\\n  - `vite-plugin-pwa` configured with start URL `/foyer`\\r\\n  - Lovable preview Service Worker guard in `src/main.tsx`\\r\\n- Identity rules: email sign-in, profile identity uses **name** (not username), instructor is displayed as **\\"Joshua\\"**, students display their real profile names.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Cross-tool instruction hygiene\\r\\n\\r\\n`CLAUDE.md` is the fuller project source of truth. `AGENTS.md` is the Codex-facing adapter and should stay aligned without becoming a second full copy.\\r\\n\\r\\n- When updating project instructions, update both files as needed: put durable detail here, and keep `AGENTS.md` as a concise adapter.\\r\\n- When creating a Claude skill that Codex should use, also copy it to `.agents/skills/` with its `SKILL.md` and supporting files.\\r\\n- When creating a Claude agent that Codex should spawn, also create a matching `.codex/agents/<name>.toml` and place the converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.\\r\\n- When adding shared knowledge, put it in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/` so every tool can read the same source.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Verification (must not skip)\\r\\n\\r\\nAny change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not \\"done\\" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + browser tool, e.g. Playwright or chrome devtools MCP), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Do not report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This has been the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions (repeated \\"it\'s not working\\" / \\"i dont see it\\" corrections) — treat it as a hard gate, not a suggestion.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** That means narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A responsive defect that gets pushed as \\"fixed\\" and does not hold costs a second full session — this has already happened (a pushed Home-page fix that did not survive, then 13 screenshots and 8 emulate cycles to actually close it). One matrix pass before the push is cheaper than either.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Product rules (must not regress)\\r\\n\\r\\n- Foyer (`FoyerSidebar` + `FoyerHeroRing`) is the primary navigation. The Foyer additionally hosts a `GooeyDock` quick-launch row well below the orbital ring; this dock is allowed only on `/foyer`. Do not add docks elsewhere.\\r\\n- Inbox is real messaging with back-and-forth threads and PDF attachments, scoped 1:1 per student with admin.\\r\\n- Admin is fully responsive on desktop and mobile.\\r\\n- Do not expose actions users cannot actually perform — no fake analytics, no dead buttons, no misleading placeholders. Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake data.\\r\\n- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Known gaps (do not invent answers)\\r\\n\\r\\n- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run them with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.\\r\\n- No Prettier config and no CI workflows. Nothing runs `typecheck`, `lint`, or the tests automatically — `.claude/safe-push.sh` is the only gate, and only for typecheck.\\r\\n- `README.md` is a stub.\\r\\n- `.claude/settings.json` is set up with a permission allowlist for safe dev work (npm scripts, git reads, MCP GitHub reads) and a denylist for destructive ops. No custom `.claude/agents/`. A `SessionStart` auto-pull hook is wired in `.claude/settings.local.json` (runs `.claude/auto-pull-hook.sh`); Codex mirrors it in `.codex/hooks.json`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Skill / agent / command vault\\r\\n\\r\\nThe full skill/agent/command vault lives in the brain root at `~/ai-os/skills/`, `~/ai-os/agents/`, `~/ai-os/commands/` (split into `claude/` and `codex/` subtrees, indexed by `~/ai-os/skills/INDEX.md`). It is inert storage — nothing here auto-loads. To use a vault skill in this project, symlink it into `.claude/skills/` (Codex: `.agents/skills/`). When you create a new skill/agent/command, save it to BOTH this project\'s `.claude/<kind>/` and the vault, so the vault stays the complete catalog.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Agent skills\\r\\n\\r\\n### Issue tracker\\r\\n\\r\\nGitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Triage labels\\r\\n\\r\\nThe five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Domain docs\\r\\n\\r\\nMulti-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. See `docs/agents/domain.md`.\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 10.2 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"f160f4","wall_time_seconds":0.3174093,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":888,"output":"#!/usr/bin/env node\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Derives public/data/rc/rc-question-index.json from the already-built RC bank\\r\\n * (rc-index.json + RC-PT<n>.json). The search page needs per-question metadata\\r\\n * (type, family, features, prompt) without loading all 59 PrepTest files; this\\r\\n * emits one flat record per drillable question.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Only questions present in the bank are indexed — those all carry a written\\r\\n * explanation and answer choices, so every search result can be drilled.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Usage: node scripts/build-rc-search-index.mjs\\r\\n */\\r\\nimport { readFileSync, writeFileSync, readdirSync } from \'node:fs\';\\r\\nimport { dirname, join } from \'node:path\';\\r\\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \'node:url\';\\r\\n\\r\\n// URL.pathname is percent-encoded and begins with `/C:/` on Windows. Convert\\r\\n// the module URL to a native filesystem path before joining input/output paths.\\r\\nconst RC_DIR = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), \'..\', \'public\', \'data\', \'rc\');\\r\\n\\r\\nconst index = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(RC_DIR, \'rc-index.json\'), \'utf8\'));\\r\\n\\r\\n// passageId -> index row, for PT/section/set stamping and cross-checking.\\r\\nconst passageMeta = new Map();\\r\\nfor (const pt of index.preptests) {\\r\\n  for (const p of pt.passages) {\\r\\n    passageMeta.set(p.passageId, {\\r\\n      preptest: pt.preptest,\\r\\n      section: p.section,\\r\\n      setNumber: p.setNumber,\\r\\n      questionIds: p.questionIds,\\r\\n    });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst entries = [];\\r\\nconst seen = new Set();\\r\\n\\r\\nconst files = readdirSync(RC_DIR)\\r\\n  .filter((f) => /^RC-PT\\\\d+\\\\.json$/.test(f))\\r\\n  .sort((a, b) => Number(a.match(/\\\\d+/)[0]) - Number(b.match(/\\\\d+/)[0]));\\r\\n\\r\\nfor (const file of files) {\\r\\n  const data = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(RC_DIR, file), \'utf8\'));\\r\\n  for (const passage of data.passages) {\\r\\n    const meta = passageMeta.get(passage.passageId);\\r\\n    if (!meta) throw new Error(`${file}: passage ${passage.passageId} missing from rc-index.json`);\\r\\n    for (const q of passage.questions) {\\r\\n      if (seen.has(q.questionId)) throw new Error(`${file}: duplicate questionId ${q.questionId}`);\\r\\n      seen.add(q.questionId);\\r\\n      entries.push({\\r\\n        questionId: q.questionId,\\r\\n        passageId: passage.passageId,\\r\\n        preptest: meta.preptest,\\r\\n        section: meta.section,\\r\\n        setNumber: meta.setNumber,\\r\\n        position: q.position,\\r\\n        questionType: q.questionType,\\r\\n        questionTypeFamily: q.questionTypeFamily,\\r\\n        difficulty: q.difficulty ?? null,\\r\\n        features: (q.features ?? []).map((f) => f.name),\\r\\n        prompt: q.prompt ?? null,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// Cross-check: the index\'s questionIds and the emitted set must agree exactly,\\r\\n// so the hub and the search page can never disagree about what is drillable.\\r\\nconst indexIds = new Set();\\r\\nfor (const { questionIds } of passageMeta.values()) {\\r\\n  for (const id of questionIds) {\\r\\n    if (indexIds.has(id)) throw new Error(`rc-index.json: duplicate questionId ${id}`);\\r\\n    indexIds.add(id);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\nconst missing = [...indexIds].filter((id) => !seen.has(id));\\r\\nconst extra = [...seen].filter((id) => !indexIds.has(id));\\r\\nif (missing.length || extra.length) {\\r\\n  throw new Error(\\r\\n    `question-id mismatch vs rc-index.json: missing=${missing.slice(0, 5)} extra=${extra.slice(0, 5)}`,\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nentries.sort((a, b) => a.questionId - b.questionId);\\r\\n\\r\\nconst out = { questionCount: entries.length, questions: entries };\\r\\nconst target = join(RC_DIR, \'rc-question-index.json\');\\r\\nwriteFileSync(target, JSON.stringify(out));\\r\\nconsole.log(`rc-search-index: wrote ${entries.length} questions to ${target}`);\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"d8938d","wall_time_seconds":0.4317331,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":9,"output":" M supabase/functions/mcp/index.ts\\n"}'}]

Ours is PT101-159 end to end — every question is modern — so a\\r\\n   Legacy tab would be a control that returns nothing every time it is pressed.\\r\\n   The switch that is real here is the one between the two banks. */\\r\\nconst BANK_TABS: { value: QuestionFinderBank | \\"all\\"; label: string }[] = [\\r\\n  { value: \\"all\\", label: \\"All\\" },\\r\\n  { value: \\"lr\\", label: \\"LR\\" },\\r\\n  { value: \\"rc\\", label: \\"RC\\" },\\r\\n];\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface QuestionFinderProps {\\r\\n  /** Where a finished one-question attempt returns to. */\\r\\n  returnTo: string;\\r\\n  /** The host\'s surface. The panel follows it so the overlay never flashes a\\r\\n   *  warm-paper card over the near-black Drill landing workspace. */\\r\\n  tone?: \\"light\\" | \\"dark\\";\\r\\n  /** Restricts results to the active side of the LR/RC hub switch. */\\r\\n  bank?: QuestionFinderBank;\\r\\n  triggerLabel?: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The Question finder.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A labelled field that opens a panel: type words to search question text, or\\r\\n * type a question address (PT90 S1 Q12) to locate it. It can search both banks\\r\\n * or follow the Drill hub\'s active bank. Selecting a result retains its address;\\r\\n * opening remains unavailable until safe preview is implemented.\\r\\n * See `docs/adr/0008-question-finder-lands-differently-by-bank.md`.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * It never builds a set. That is the Drill console\'s job, and the last row of\\r\\n * the panel is the door to it.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport interface QuestionFinderHandle {\\r\\n  open: () => void;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst QuestionFinder = React.forwardRef<QuestionFinderHandle, QuestionFinderProps>(function QuestionFinder({ returnTo: _returnTo, tone = \\"light\\", bank, triggerLabel = \\"Find a question\\" }, ref) {\\r\\n  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);\\r\\n  React.useImperativeHandle(ref, () => ({ open: () => setOpen(true) }), []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  /* Cmd/Ctrl+K is an accelerator on top of a visible, labelled control — never\\r\\n     the only way in. It is ignored while the student is typing somewhere else,\\r\\n     so it cannot steal a keystroke from a real field.\\r\\n\\r\\n     \\"/\\" opens it too, the convention everywhere else a search box lives behind\\r\\n     a key. It only opens, it does not toggle: \\"/\\" while the panel is open is a\\r\\n     character someone is trying to type. */\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {\\r\\n      const target = event.target as HTMLElement | null;\\r\\n      const tag = target?.tagName;\\r\\n      const typing = tag === \\"INPUT\\" || tag === \\"TEXTAREA\\" || target?.isContentEditable === true;\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (event.key === \\"/\\" && !event.metaKey && !event.ctrlKey && !event.altKey) {\\r\\n        if (open || typing) return;\\r\\n        event.preventDefault();\\r\\n        setOpen(true);\\r\\n        return;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (event.key.toLowerCase() !== \\"k\\" || !(event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey)) return;\\r\\n      if (!open && typing) return;\\r\\n      event.preventDefault();\\r\\n      setOpen((value) => !value);\\r\\n    };\\r\\n    window.addEventListener(\\"keydown\\", onKey);\\r\\n    return () => window.removeEventListener(\\"keydown\\", onKey);\\r\\n  }, [open]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"qf\\" data-tone={tone}>\\r\\n      {/* ONE trigger, not one per breakpoint. Two buttons behind a CSS\\r\\n          display swap would both stay in the accessibility tree, and a screen\\r\\n          reader would announce \\"Find a question\\" twice on every visit. The shortcut still works but is not advertised\\r\\n          on the trigger: Joshua\'s call, 2026-09-08. */}\\r\\n      <button\\r\\n        type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n        className=\\"qf-trigger\\"\\r\\n        onClick={() => setOpen(true)}\\r\\n        aria-haspopup=\\"dialog\\"\\r\\n        aria-expanded={open}\\r\\n        aria-label={triggerLabel}\\r\\n        /* The shortcuts are declared, not drawn. \\"all\\"), [bank]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const results = React.useMemo(\\r\\n    () =>\\r\\n      runFinderSearch({\\r\\n        query,\\r\\n        lr: banks.lr,\\r\\n        rcPassages: banks.rcPassages,\\r\\n        rcQuestions: banks.rcQuestions,\\r\\n        limit: PER_BANK_LIMIT,\\r\\n        bank: view === \\"all\\" ? undefined : view,\\r\\n      }),\\r\\n    [view, banks.lr, banks.rcPassages, banks.rcQuestions, query],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  const hits = React.useMemo<FinderHit[]>(\\r\\n    () => [...results.lr, ...results.rc],\\r\\n    [results.lr, results.rc],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => setActive(0), [query]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  /* Focus in, focus back out. The trigger must get focus again on close or a\\r\\n     keyboard student is returned to the top of the document. */\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (!open) return;\\r\\n    const previous = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;\\r\\n    inputRef.current?.focus();\\r\\n    const { overflow } = document.body.style;\\r\\n    document.body.style.overflow = \\"hidden\\";\\r\\n    return () => {\\r\\n      document.body.style.overflow = overflow;\\r\\n      previous?.focus?.();\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }, [open]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const openHit = React.useCallback((hit: FinderHit) => {\\r\\n    setView(hit.bank);\\r\\n    setQuery(hit.bank === \\"lr\\" ? `PT${hit.pt} S${hit.section} Q${hit.qnum}` : `PT${hit.preptest} S${hit.section}${hit.qnum ? ` Q${hit.qnum}` : \\"\\"}`);\\r\\n  }, []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const onKeyDown = (event: React.KeyboardEvent) => {\\r\\n    if (event.key === \\"Escape\\") {\\r\\n      event.preventDefault();\\r\\n      // Escape clears a query before it closes the panel — the same two-step the\\r\\n      // old drill console used, so a mistyped search is one key from fixed.\\r\\n      if (query) setQuery(\\"\\");\\r\\n      else onClose();\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    if (event.key === \\"ArrowDown\\") {\\r\\n      event.preventDefault();\\r\\n      setActive((index) => (hits.length ? (index + 1) % hits.length : 0));\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    if (event.key === \\"ArrowUp\\") {\\r\\n      event.preventDefault();\\r\\n      setActive((index) => (hits.length ? (index - 1 + hits.length) % hits.length : 0));\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    if (event.key === \\"Enter\\") {\\r\\n      const hit = hits[active];\\r\\n      if (hit) {\\r\\n        event.preventDefault();\\r\\n        openHit(hit);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    if (event.key === \\"Tab\\") {\\r\\n      // A modal that leaks focus to the page behind it is not modal. Cycle\\r\\n      // within the panel rather than trapping the student on one control.\\r\\n      const focusable = panelRef.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(\\r\\n        \'button, input, select, [href], [tabindex]:not([tabindex=\\"-1\\"])\',\\r\\n      );\\r\\n      if (!focusable?.length) return;\\r\\n      const first = focusable[0];\\r\\n      const last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];\\r\\n      if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {\\r\\n        event.preventDefault();\\r\\n        last.focus();\\r\\n      } else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {\\r\\n        event.preventDefault();\\r\\n        first.focus();\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Keep the highlighted row in view when it moves by keyboard.\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    const row = listRef.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(\'[data-active=\\"true\\"]\');\\r\\n    // Optional call, not optional chain on the element: jsdom has no\\r\\n    // scrollIntoView, and keyboard navigation must not depend on it existing.\\r\\n    row?.scrollIntoView?.({ block: \\"nearest\\" });\\r\\n  }, [active]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const hasQuery = query.trim().length > 0;\\r\\n  const showNoResults = hasQuery && !banks.loading && hits.length === 0;\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!open) return null;\\r\\n  const panel = (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"qf\\" data-tone={tone}>\\r\\n      <div className=\\"qf-portal\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"qf-scrim\\" onClick={onClose} aria-hidden />\\r\\n        <div\\r\\n          className=\\"qf-panel\\"\\r\\n          ref={panelRef}\\r\\n          role=\\"dialog\\"\\r\\n          aria-modal=\\"true\\"\\r\\n          aria-label=\\"Find a question\\"\\r\\n          onKeyDown={onKeyDown}\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          {/* The panel names itself and carries its own close, the way LSAT\\r\\n              Lab\'s does. The scrim still closes it; a titled dialog just\\r\\n              should not depend on the student knowing that. */}\\r\\n          <div className=\\"qf-head\\">\\r\\n            <h2 className=\\"qf-title\\">Question Finder</h2>\\r\\n            <button\\r\\n              type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n              className=\\"qf-close\\"\\r\\n              onClick={onClose}\\r\\n              aria-label=\\"Close the question finder\\"\\r\\n            >\\r\\n              <X size={17} strokeWidth={2} aria-hidden />\\r\\n            </button>\\r\\n          </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n          <div className=\\"qf-controls\\">\\r\\n            <div className=\\"qf-banks\\" role=\\"group\\" aria-label=\\"Which bank to search\\">\\r\\n              {BANK_TABS.map((tab) => (\\r\\n                <button\\r\\n                  key={tab.value}\\r\\n                  type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n                  className=\\"qf-bank\\"\\r\\n                  data-on={view === tab.value}\\r\\n                  aria-pressed={view === tab.value}\\r\\n                  onClick={() => setView(tab.value)}\\r\\n                >\\r\\n                  {tab.label}\\r\\n                </button>\\r\\n              ))}\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n            {/* The three selectors lead now, rather than hiding behind a\\r\\n                disclosure.      selectedAddress={results.address}\\r\\n            />\\r\\n            {addressIndex.failed && <p className=\\"qf-warn\\" role=\\"alert\\">Question addresses could not be loaded. <button type=\\"button\\" onClick={addressIndex.retry}>Retry</button></p>}\\r\\n\\r\\n          <div className=\\"qf-inputrow\\">\\r\\n            <Search className=\\"qf-inputrow-icon\\" size={18} > 0 && (\\r\\n              <div className=\\"qf-group\\">\\r\\n                <p className=\\"qf-grouphead\\">\\r\\n                  Logical Reasoning\\r\\n                  <i aria-hidden />\\r\\n       <span className=\\"qf-row-meta\\">\\r\\n                      <span className=\\"qf-tag\\">{hit.qtype}</span>\\r\\n                      <ArrowRight className=\\"qf-row-go\\" size={14} Elsewhere both banks are\\r\\n * available. Either way browsing and typing use the same resolver.\\r\\n */\\r\\nfunction BrowseByTest({\\r\\n  bank,\\r\\n  disabled,\\r\\n  onPick,\\r\\n  tests,\\r\\n  selectedAddress,\\r\\n}: {\\r\\n  bank?: QuestionFinderBank;\\r\\n  disabled: boolean;\\r\\n  onPick: (query: string) => void;\\r\\n  tests: FinderAddressTest[];\\r\\n  selectedAddress: QuestionAddress | null;\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  const [pt, setPt] = React.useState(\\"\\");\\r\\n  const [section, setSection] = React.useState(\\"\\");\\r\\n  /* The question keeps its choice like the other two. []).flatMap((passage) => passage.questions.map((question) => question.number));\\r\\n  }, [tests, pt, section]);\\r\\n  const pick = React.useCallback((nextPt: string, nextSection: string, nextQnum: string) => {\\r\\n    if (!nextPt) return onPick(\\"\\");\\r\\n    let query = `PT${nextPt}`;\\r\\n    if (nextSection) query += ` S${nextSection}`;\\r\\n    if (nextSection && nextQnum) query += ` Q${nextQnum}`;\\r\\n    onPick(query);\\r\\n  }, [onPick]);\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (bank === \\"rc\\" && sections.length === 1 && !section) {\\r\\n      setSection(String(sections[0]));\\r\\n      pick(pt, String(sections[0]), \\"\\");\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }, [bank, sections, section, pt, pick]);\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    setPt(\\"\\");\\r\\n    setSection(\\"\\");\\r\\n    setQnum(\\"\\");\\r\\n  }, [bank]);\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (!selectedAddress) return;\\r\\n    setPt(String(selectedAddress.pt));\\r\\n    setSection(selectedAddress.section ? String(selectedAddress.section) : \\"\\");\\r\\n    setQnum(selectedAddress.qnum ? [];\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"qf-browse\\">\\r\\n      <label className=\\"qf-field\\">\\r\\n        <span className=\\"qf-field-label\\">Test</span>\\r\\n        <select\\r\\n          className=\\"qf-select\\"\\r\\n          value={pt}\\r\\n          disabled={disabled}\\r\\n          onChange={(event) => {\\r\\n            setPt(event.target.value);\\r\\n            setSection(\\"\\");\\r\\n            setQnum(\\"\\");\\r\\n            pick(event.target.value, \\"\\", \\"\\");\\r\\n          }}\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          <option value=\\"\\">{disabled ? \\"Loading…\\" : \\"PrepTest\\"}</option>\\r\\n          {pts.map((value) => (\\r\\n            <option key={value} value={value}>\\r\\n              PT {value}\\r\\n            </option>\\r\\n          ))}\\r\\n        </select>\\r\\n      </label>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <label className=\\"qf-field\\">\\r\\n        <span className=\\"qf-field-label\\">Section</span>\\r\\n        <select\\r\\n          className=\\"qf-select\\"\\r\\n          value={section}\\r\\n          disabled={disabled || !pt}\\r\\n          onChange={(event) => {\\r\\n            setSection(event.target.value);\\r\\n            setQnum(\\"\\");\\r\\n            pick(pt, event.target.value, \\"\\");\\r\\n          }}\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          <option value=\\"\\">{pt ? \\"Section\\" : \\"Pick a test\\"}</option>\\r\\n          {sections.map((value) => (\\r\\n            <option key={value} value={value}>\\r\\n              Section {value}\\r\\n            </option>\\r\\n          ))}\\r\\n        </select>\\r\\n      </label>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <label className=\\"qf-field\\">\\r\\n        <span className=\\"qf-field-label\\">Question</span>\\r\\n        <Select\\r\\n          value={qnum}\\r\\n          disabled={disabled || !section || qnums.length === 0}\\r\\n          onValueChange={(value) => { setQnum(value); pick(pt, section, value); }}\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          <SelectTrigger className=\\"qf-select-radix\\" aria-label=\\"Question\\">\\r\\n            <SelectValue placeholder={!section ? \\"Pick a section\\" : qnums.length === 0 ? \\"No questions\\" : \\"Question\\"} />\\r\\n          </SelectTrigger>\\r\\n          <SelectContent className=\\"qf-question-menu\\" position=\\"popper\\" side=\\"bottom\\" align=\\"start\\">\\r\\n            {rcGroups.length ? `, ${passage.title}` : \\"\\"}`}>\\r\\n                    Question {question.number}\\r\\n                  </SelectItem>)}\\r\\n                </SelectGroup>\\r\\n              </React.Fragment>;\\r\\n            }) : qnums.map((value) => <SelectItem key={value} value={String(value)} aria-label={`PT ${pt} Section ${section} Question ${value}`}>Question {value}</SelectItem>)}\\r\\n          </SelectContent>\\r\\n        </Select>\\r\\n      </label>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <button\\r\\n        type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n        className=\\"qf-go\\"\\r\\n        disabled\\r\\n        aria-label=\\"Open question unavailable until preview is ready\\"\\r\\n        title=\\"Question preview will be available in the next step.\\"\\r\\n      >\\r\\n        Open question (not available yet)\\r\\n      </button>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 8.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"82df49","wall_time_seconds":0.4740666,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":764,"output":"/* ── Question finder ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\\r\\n   One control that turns something a student can say about a question into\\r\\n   that question opened. Mounted on Classroom Home and in the Drill landing\\r\\n   workspace, so it carries its own token block rather than inheriting either\\r\\n   host\'s palette: the trigger takes the host\'s tone, the panel floats above\\r\\n   the page and always looks like itself.\\r\\n\\r\\n   Follows the Classroom\'s rules, because Home is where it is seen most:\\r\\n   every hairline is a box-shadow ring rather than a border, so a hover state\\r\\n   cannot shift anything by a pixel; every animation is transform/opacity only,\\r\\n   so nothing here can reflow while it moves. */\\r\\n\\r\\n.qf {\\r\\n  --qf-ink: #332f2c;\\r\\n  --qf-ink-mid: #5c5751;\\r\\n  --qf-body: #6b6660;\\r\\n  --qf-soft: #8a857f;\\r\\n  --qf-muted: #a7a29f;\\r\\n\\r\\n  --qf-surface: #ffffff;\\r\\n  --qf-surface-warm: #fffdf9;\\r\\n  --qf-row-hover: #fdf7ef;\\r\\n  --qf-sunk: #f8f4ee;\\r\\n\\r\\n  --qf-accent: #ff8906;\\r\\n  --qf-accent-text: #e5730a;\\r\\n  --qf-gold: #c5820a;\\r\\n\\r\\n  --qf-hair-06: rgba(68, 64, 61, 0.06);\\r\\n  --qf-hair-08: rgba(68, 64, 61, 0.08);\\r\\n  --qf-hair-12: rgba(68, 64, 61, 0.12);\\r\\n\\r\\n  --qf-ui: \\"Outfit\\", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;\\r\\n  --qf-mono: \\"JetBrains Mono\\", ui-monospace, monospace;\\r\\n  --qf-ease: cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* The Drill landing workspace is a near-black surface. Its trigger takes the\\r\\n   dark tone directly; the panel gets it through the portal so the overlay does\\r\\n   not flash a warm-paper card over a black page. */\\r\\n.qf[data-tone=\\"dark\\"],\\r\\nhtml.dark .qf {\\r\\n  --qf-ink: #f5f1ec;\\r\\n  --qf-ink-mid: #cfc8c0;\\r\\n  --qf-body: #b3aca4;\\r\\n  --qf-soft: #9b948d;\\r\\n  --qf-muted: ## assistant

**One material finding, P2: portaled passage groups lose their visual tokens.**

[QuestionFinder.tsx:639](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu/src/components/questionfinder/QuestionFinder.tsx:639) renders the question menu through shared `SelectContent`, which portals directly beneath `document.body`. It supplies `qf-question-menu` but no `.qf` token scope or tone.

The new [passage heading and separator styles](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu/src/components/questionfinder/questionFinder.css:786) use `--qf-sunk`, `--qf-ink-mid`, `--qf-mono`, and `--qf-hair-12`. These variables exist only on `.qf` ancestors, so the portaled menu cannot inherit them. Heading tint, typography, and divider appearance consequently fail to match the design, including dark mode.

**Fix:** propagate the finder’s token scope and tone onto the portaled menu, or use globally available theme tokens. Verify computed colors and visible boundaries in both themes.

No other material standards findings. The existing dialog keyboard interception should be tested with the native Test/Section selects and new Radix Question select; it predates this commit and is not counted as a new regression here.

Working-tree status remains only the unrelated, unstaged MCP modification. No files changed or tests executed.

## assistant

One material issue remains: **bank changes can restore a selection that was supposed to clear.**

The unique-RC-section effect runs before the bank-reset and address-sync effects. Example:

1. In **All**, select PT140 without selecting a section.
2. Switch to **RC**.
3. The parent clears the query, but the auto-selection effect still sees the previous local `pt=140`.
4. It writes `PT140 S4`, which restores the previous test selection.

Gate auto-selection on the validated selected address matching the local test, or derive automatic section selection from the validated address instead of stale local state.

The portal token fix, selector keyboard isolation, and disabled-state guard for retry preservation otherwise look sound. No edits or tests performed.

## assistant

**No remaining material findings in the current source changes.**

The new `selectedAddress?.pt === Number(pt)` guard closes the bank-switch race: clearing the authoritative query prevents stale local test state from triggering automatic RC section selection.

The implementation remains reasonably maintainable:

- Query/address synchronization validates against available sections and questions.
- Retry loading preserves existing selection until the index becomes available.
- Close/reopen preserves query and address state.
- Selector keys remain owned by their controls.
- Portaled content now receives finder token scope and tone.

The nested question-number expression is dense but does not justify another refactor for this bounded repair. No edits or tests performed; this is source review, not live interaction acceptance.

Replace the oversized competing hero/embedded selector with compact Create RC drill and Open a question entries. Keep unrelated assignments in My Work or a clearly secondary collapsed area on this surface; keep them absent inside active setup. Give inactive tabs readable text and distinct selected/focus states.\\r\\n\\r\\nPreserve18px /1.6 defaults, responsive passage-only width and current saved text-size behavior. Add announced selected states to spacing and color options using existing radio/pressed patterns. Keep the popup bounded and scrollable, with focus returning to its trigger. Shared LR toolbar defaults must survive.\\r\\n\\r\\nExisting seams: AcademyMainhub.section.test.tsx, OfficialDrillToolbar.test.tsx, useRcA11y.test.ts, OfficialSmartDrillBar.test.tsx.\\r\\n\\r\\n- [ ] Create and lookup are visually distinct and have one clear action each.\\r\\n- [ ] In progress/Completed/Saved sets remain readable against the actual light background.\\r\\n- [ ] Spacing/color changes are announced and retained across reload; malformed saved settings recover.\\r\\n- [ ] Long standard/comparative passages remain readable without horizontal text scroll.\\r\\n- [ ] Browser matrix and200% zoom/text resize show no lost controls, clipped popup or hidden selected state.\\r\\n- [ ] Reproduce the original clipping condition before declaring its cause/fix; report unresolved conditions precisely.\\r\\n\\r\\nAvoid a cosmetic test for each class name. Kept here as the default so the LR\\r\\n * player\'s call sites stay unchanged; RC passes its own set.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport const LR_DIRECTIONS: ToolbarDirections = {\\r\\n  title: \\"Directions\\",\\r\\n  srDescription: \\"Directions for the LSAT Logical Reasoning section.\\",\\r\\n  // LSAC\'s current digital wording, the same text the section\'s directions\\r\\n  // page shows. The old paper-test version told students to blacken a space\\r\\n  // on an answer sheet they do not have.\\r\\n  body: <p>{SECTION_DIRECTIONS.lr}</p>,\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface FontScaleOption {\\r\\n  key: string;\\r\\n  label: string;\\r\\n  value: number;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface Props {\\r\\n  highlightMode: HighlightMode;\\r\\n  setHighlightMode: (mode: HighlightMode) => void;\\r\\n  /** Pre-formatted timer label, e.g. \\"20:41\\". Empty string when no timer. */\\r\\n  timerLabel: string;\\r\\n  /** Actual timer mode — drives label prefix exactly. Undefined hides timer box. */\\r\\n  timerMode?: \\"countdown\\" | \\"stopwatch\\";\\r\\n  /** Paint the timer as overrun. Map those\\r\\n * forward so a student who already picked one keeps roughly what they chose.\\r\\n */\\r\\nfunction migrateLineHeight(stored: number | undefined): number {\\r\\n  if (stored == null) return DEFAULTS.lineHeight;\\r\\n  if (stored < 1) return stored;\\r\\n  if (stored >= 1.8) return 0.45;\\r\\n  if (stored >= 1.5) return 0.25;\\r\\n  return 0;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction read(): RcA11ySettings {\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\\r\\n    if (!raw) return DEFAULTS;\\r\\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<RcA11ySettings>;\\r\\n    return {\\r\\n      scheme: parsed.scheme ?? [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 11.9 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"2ae21f","wall_time_seconds":0.6386832,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":408,"output":"}\\r\\n\\r\\n.drill-observatory-root .drill-observatory-canvas {\\r\\n  isolation: isolate;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* ── Shared micro-typography ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */\\r\\n\\r\\n/* Tailwind\'s `font-mono` stack does not include the label face, so anything\\r\\n   using it landed on ui-monospace — a different face from the labels beside it.\\r\\n   The Today panel\'s date sat directly next to \\"TODAY\\" in two different monos. */\\r\\n.drill-observatory-root .font-mono {\\r\\n  font-family: var(--observatory-label-font);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* One treatment for every uppercase micro-label in the observatory. Applied via\\r\\n   an explicit class rather than a blanket `.uppercase` selector — the\\r\\n   observatory hosts launcher stages whose labels are not part of this set. */\\r\\n.drill-observatory-root .drill-observatory-label {\\r\\n  color: var(--observatory-ink-50);\\r\\n  font-family: var(--observatory-label-font);\\r\\n  font-size: var(--observatory-text-label);\\r\\n  font-weight: 500;\\r\\n  letter-spacing: var(--observatory-track-label);\\r\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* The dateline prints twice and CSS picks one, so the first paint is already\\r\\n   right. Between 900 and 1099 the greeting column is too narrow for the long\\r\\n   month and the date wrapped to two lines. */\\r\\n.drill-observatory-root .drill-observatory-date-short {\\r\\n  display: none;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n@media (min-width: 900px) and (max-width: 1099px) {\\r\\n  .drill-observatory-root .drill-observatory-date-long {\\r\\n    display: none;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  /* The short month alone was not enough: at 1024 the greeting column is 201px\\r\\n"}'}]

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu/src/features/rc/hooks/useRcA11y.test.ts\n@@\n it.each([0, -100, '125', 9999])('recovers a usable default from an invalid saved text size %s', size => {\n@@\n });\n+\n+it.each([-1, 9999, '0.25', null])('recovers defaults from malformed spacing and scheme %s', lineHeight => {\n+  localStorage.setItem('rc-player-a11y', JSON.stringify({ scheme: 'unknown', lineHeight }));\n+  const { result } = renderHook(() => useRcA11y());\n+  expect(result.current.settings.scheme).toBe('default');\n+  expect(result.current.extraLeading).toBe(0);\n+});\n+\n+it('retains spacing and color across reload and migrates legacy spacing', () => {\n+  localStorage.setItem('rc-player-a11y', JSON.stringify({ lineHeight: 1.65 }));\n+  const first = renderHook(() => useRcA11y());\n+  expect(first.result.current.extraLeading).toBe(0.25);\n+  act(() => first.result.current.update({ lineHeight: 0.45, scheme: 'navy' }));\n+  first.unmount();\n+  const restored = renderHook(() => useRcA11y());\n+  expect(restored.result.current.settings).toEqual({ fontSize: 100, lineHeight: 0.45, scheme: 'navy' });\n+});\n*** End Patch"));

Session and device writes begin exclusively on Start. */\\r\\nexport default function RcQuestionPreview() {\\r\\n  const [params] = useSearchParams();\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const actor = React.useRef(user?.id);\\r\\n  actor.current = user?.id;\\r\\n  const passageId = Number(params.get(\'passage\'));\\r\\n  const questionId = Number(params.get(\'q\'));\\r\\n  const back = safeRcReturnTo(params.get(\'returnTo\')) ?? FALLBACK;\\r\\n  const [passage, setPassage] = React.useState<RcPassage | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [error, setError] = React.useState(\'\');\\r\\n  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(true);\\r\\n  const [retry, setRetry] = React.useState(0);\\r\\n  const [starting, setStarting] = React.useState(false);\\r\\n  const busy = React.useRef(false);\\r\\n  const mounted = React.useRef(true);\\r\\n  const pending = React.useRef<Pending | null>(null);\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => { pending.current = null; }, [user?.id, passageId, questionId]);\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => { mounted.current = true; return () => { mounted.current = false; }; }, []);\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    let cancelled = false;\\r\\n    setLoading(true); setPassage(null); setError(\'\');\\r\\n    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(passageId) || passageId <= 0 || !Number.isSafeInteger(questionId) || questionId <= 0) {\\r\\n      setError(\'That question address is invalid.\'); setLoading(false); return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    loadPassage(passageId).then((loaded) => {\\r\\n      if (cancelled) return;\\r\\n      if (!loaded || !loaded.questions.some((q) => q.questionId === questionId)) throw new Error(\'That question is not in this passage.\');\\r\\n      setPassage(loaded);\\r\\n    }).catch((err: unknown) => { if (!cancelled) setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : \'The preview could not load.\'); })\\r\\n      .finally(() => { if (!cancelled) setLoading(false); });\\r\\n    return () => { cancelled = true; };\\r\\n  }, [passageId, questionId, retry]);\\r\\n  const start = async () => {\\r\\n    if (!passage || busy.current) return;\\r\\n    busy.current = true; setStarting(true); setError(\'\');\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      if (!user) throw new Error(\'Sign in to start a drill.\');\\r\\n      const raw = sessionStorage.getItem(pendingKey(user.id));\\r\\n      if (!pending.current && raw) {\\r\\n        const saved = JSON.parse(raw) as Pending;\\r\\n        if (typeof saved.id !== \'string\' || saved.actorId !== user.id || saved.passageId !== passageId || saved.questionId !== questionId || safeRcReturnTo(saved.back) !== saved.back) {\\r\\n          throw new Error(\'A different passage launch is awaiting confirmation. Return to that preview before starting another drill.\');\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        pending.current = saved;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      const request = pending.current ?? { id: crypto.randomUUID(), actorId: user.id, passageId, questionId, back };\\r\\n      if (request.actorId !== actor.current) throw new Error(\'The signed-in account changed.\');\\r\\n      pending.current = request;\\r\\n      sessionStorage.setItem(pendingKey(request.actorId), JSON.stringify(request));\\r\\n      const verifyActor = () => { if (actor.current !== request.actorId) throw new Error(\'The signed-in account changed.\'); };\\r\\n      let saved: RcDrillSession | null;\\r\\n      try { saved = await getSession(request.id, request.actorId); }\\r\\n      catch { throw new Error(\'We could not confirm the launch. ## assistant

Ticket04 implementation is ready in eight owned files.

- RC landing now has one compact **Create RC drill** action, separate lookup, and collapsed assigned work.
- Inactive tabs use readable stage colors and explicit focus outlines.
- Reading preferences return focus to their trigger.
- Invalid saved color/spacing settings recover; known legacy spacing migrates.
- LR hero and default font choices remain intact.

**Checks:** 38/38 focused tests passed. Targeted ESLint passed with two existing Fast Refresh warnings. No commit, push, or deployment.

**Ticket03 review finding:** `RcQuestionPreview.tsx:67–84` checks actor and unmount state, but not changed passage/question parameters during an asynchronous launch. A pending request can navigate into the previous passage and clear its recovery record after another preview loads. Add a request-context guard and deferred-launch route-change test.

Integrated browser matrix, 200% resizing, and original clipping reproduction remain pending.

Rename Set Number/Difficulty to Passage/Passage difficulty and use digital answer-choice directions.\\r\\n\\r\\nProvide a compact passage-grouped question overview when the existing pager exceeds usable narrow-screen space. Show answered/unanswered/flagged status; expose correctness only after the configured reveal boundary. Preserve current question focus, passage scroll and keyboard navigation.\\r\\n\\r\\nUse ticket02\'s passage-heading/divider design consistently: labelled groups and visible spacing/rules between passages. A compact horizontal pager may use short vertical separators with accessible passage-group names; the expanded overview uses passage headings and horizontal dividers. Keep official numbering separate from drill ordinal, including mixed drills. Group boundaries must remain visible when navigation wraps on narrow screens.\\r\\n\\r\\nExplain protected source answers separately from any personal tools the mode permits. An in-progress session requested as review must not silently resume its timer under a read-only promise. Use an explicit route outcome consistent with ticket03.\\r\\n\\r\\nExisting seams: RcPlayer.saveSafety.test.tsx, RcPlayer.studyAids.test.tsx, rcSessionDestination.test.ts, protected history/results and shared footer tests.\\r\\n\\r\\n- [ ] A mixed drill\'s ordinal never appears as the official question number.\\r\\n- [ ] Details, breadcrumb and overview agree on the selected source address.\\r\\n- [ ] Narrow navigation preserves focus and passage position without answer leakage.\\r\\n- [ ] Protected answer/flag/clock/completion restrictions are enforced and clearly described.\\r\\n- [ ] Review of in-progress content causes no silent timer/answer mutation.\\r\\n\\r\\nKeep correctness-key repair outside this ticket.\\r\\n"}'}]

Passage Only View sits there in the official interface.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function RcToolbarButton({\\r\\n  children,\\r\\n  onClick,\\r\\n  ...buttonProps\\r\\n}: {\\r\\n  children: React.ReactNode;\\r\\n  onClick: () => void;\\r\\n} & Pick<React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>, \'aria-pressed\'>) {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <button\\r\\n      type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n      onClick={onClick}\\r\\n      {...buttonProps}\\r\\n      className=\\"rounded-sm border border-slate-500 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-[14px] font-semibold text-slate-900 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100\\"\\r\\n    >\\r\\n      {children}\\r\\n    </button>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** An icon button using the toolbar\'s own active/idle treatment. */\\r\\nexport function RcToolbarIcon({\\r\\n  label,\\r\\n  active,\\r\\n  disabled,\\r\\n  onClick,\\r\\n  children,\\r\\n}: {\\r\\n  label: string;\\r\\n  active?: boolean;\\r\\n  disabled?: boolean;\\r\\n  onClick?: () => void;\\r\\n  children: React.ReactNode;\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <button\\r\\n      type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n      onClick={onClick}\\r\\n      disabled={disabled}\\r\\n      aria-pressed={active}\\r\\n      aria-label={label}\\r\\n      title={label}\\r\\n      className={cn(\\r\\n        \'flex items-center justify-center rounded-sm p-1.5 text-slate-800 transition-colors\',\\r\\n        active\\r\\n          ? \'border border-slate-700 bg-white shadow-[inset_0_2px_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.18)]\'\\r\\n          : \'border border-transparent hover:bg-slate-200\',\\r\\n        disabled && \'cursor-not-allowed opacity-35 hover:bg-transparent\',\\r\\n      )}\\r\\n    >\\r\\n      {children}\\r\\n    </button>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ pager */\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Spoken suffix for each pill verdict, so screen readers get the status too. */\\r\\nconst PILL_STATUS_LABEL: Record<\'correct\' | \'incorrect\' | \'answered\' | \'unanswered\', string> = {\\r\\n  correct: \', correct\',\\r\\n  incorrect: \', incorrect\',\\r\\n  answered: \', answered\',\\r\\n  unanswered: \', unanswered\',\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface PagerProps {\\r\\n  entries: RcPagerEntry[];\\r\\n  currentIndex: number;\\r\\n  /** Extra classes for a question pill: answered / correct / incorrect. */\\r\\n  pillState: (entry: RcPagerEntry) => string;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * The pill\'s verdict as a VALUE, not a colour. green-600 and red-500 are not\\r\\n   * separable under deuteranopia, so the pager cannot encode correctness with\\r\\n   * fill alone — it needs a glyph and a spoken status too.\\r\\n   * See docs/chart-palette.md §3.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  pillStatus: (entry: RcPagerEntry) => \'correct\' | \'incorrect\' | \'answered\' | \'unanswered\';\\r\\n  isFlagged: (entry: RcPagerEntry) => boolean;\\r\\n  onNavigate: (index: number) => void;\\r\\n  onPrev: () => void;\\r\\n  onNext: () => void;\\r\\n  canPrev: boolean;\\r\\n  canNext: boolean;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Every question in the session as a numbered circle, with a rule between\\r\\n * passages and chevron buttons at the right — the official RC interface\'s\\r\\n * pager. The LR player has none, which is why this lives in RC.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function RcPager({\\r\\n  entries,\\r\\n  currentIndex,\\r\\n  pillState,\\r\\n  pillStatus,\\r\\n  isFlagged,\\r\\n  onNavigate,\\r\\n  onPrev,\\r\\n  onNext,\\r\\n  canPrev,\\r\\n  canNext,\\r\\n}: PagerProps) {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 pb-3\\">\\r\\n      <details className=\\"w-full rounded border border-slate-300 bg-white p-2 text-slate-900\\">\\r\\n        <summary className=\\"cursor-pointer text-sm font-medium\\">Question overview</summary>\\r\\n        {Array.from(new Set(entries.filter((entry) => entry.kind === \'question\').map((entry) => entry.passageId))).map((passageId) => {\\r\\n          const group = entries.filter((entry) => entry.kind === \'question\' && entry.passageId === passageId);\\r\\n          return <section key={passageId} aria-label={group[0]?.passageLabel ?? `Passage ${passageId}`} className=\\"mt-3 border-t border-slate-300 pt-3\\">\\r\\n            <h3 className=\\"rounded bg-slate-100 p-2 text-sm font-semibold\\">{group[0]?.passageLabel ?? `Passage ${passageId}`}</h3>\\r\\n            <div className=\\"flex flex-wrap gap-2 py-2\\">{group.map((entry) => <button type=\\"button\\" key={entry.questionId} onClick={() => onNavigate(entry.index)} aria-current={entry.index === currentIndex ? \'true\' : undefined} className={cn(\'rounded border px-3 py-2 text-sm\', entry.index === currentIndex ? \'border-slate-900 bg-slate-900 text-white\' : pillState(entry))}>\\r\\n              {entry.sourceLabel ?? `Question ${entry.label}`} · Drill {entry.label}{PILL_STATUS_LABEL[pillStatus(entry)]}{isFlagged(entry) ? \', flagged\' : \'\'}\\r\\n            </button>)}</div>\\r\\n          </section>;\\r\\n        })}\\r\\n      </details>\\r\\n      <div className=\\"flex flex-1 flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-1.5\\">\\r\\n        {entries.map((entry, i) => {\\r\\n          if (entry.kind === \'divider\') {\\r\\n            return (\\r\\n              <span\\r\\n                key={`divider-${entry.passageId}-${i}`}\\r\\n                aria-hidden\\r\\n                className=\\"mx-1.5 h-6 w-px bg-slate-300\\"\\r\\n              />\\r\\n            );\\r\\n          }\\r\\n          const isCurrent = entry.index === currentIndex;\\r\\n          return (\\r\\n            <button\\r\\n              key={`q-${entry.questionId}`}\\r\\n              type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n              onClick={() => onNavigate(entry.index)}\\r\\n              aria-label={`${entry.sourceLabel ?? `Question ${entry.label}`} · Drill ${entry.label}${PILL_STATUS_LABEL[pillStatus(entry)]}${\\r\\n                isFlagged(entry) ? \', flagged\' : \'\'\\r\\n              }`}\\r\\n              aria-current={isCurrent ? \'true\' : undefined}\\r\\n              className={cn(\\r\\n                \'relative flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border text-[13px] font-medium tabular-nums transition-colors\',\\r\\n                isCurrent\\r\\n                  ? \'border-slate-900 bg-slate-900 text-white\'\\r\\n                  : pillState(entry),\\r\\n              )}\\r\\n            >\\r\\n              {entry.label}\\r\\n              {(() => {\\r\\n                const status = pillStatus(entry);\\r\\n                if (status !== \'correct\' && status !== \'incorrect\') return null;\\r\\n                return (\\r\\n                  <span\\r\\n                    aria-hidden\\r\\n                    className={cn(\\r\\n                      \'absolute -bottom-0.5 -left-0.5 flex h-3 w-3 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-white\',\\r\\n                      status === \'correct\' ? \'bg-green-600\' : \'bg-red-500\',\\r\\n                    )}\\r\\n                  >\\r\\n                    {status === \'correct\' ? (\\r\\n                      <Check size={8} strokeWidth={4} className=\\"text-white\\" />\\r\\n                    ) : (\\r\\n                      <X size={8} strokeWidth={4} className=\\"text-white\\" />\\r\\n                    )}\\r\\n                  </span>\\r\\n                );\\r\\n              })()}\\r\\n              {isFlagged(entry) && (\\r\\n                <span\\r\\n                  aria-hidden\\r\\n                  className=\\"absolute -right-0.5 -top-0.5 h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-amber-500\\"\\r\\n                />\\r\\n              )}\\r\\n            </button>\\r\\n          );\\r\\n        })}\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <div className=\\"flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5\\">\\r\\n        <button\\r\\n          type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n          onClick={onPrev}\\r\\n          disabled={!canPrev}\\r\\n          aria-label=\\"Previous question\\"\\r\\n          className=\\"flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-slate-400 bg-white text-slate-700 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40\\"\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          <ChevronLeft size={18} />\\r\\n        </button>\\r\\n        <button\\r\\n          type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n          onClick={onNext}\\r\\n          disabled={!canNext}\\r\\n          aria-label=\\"Next question\\"\\r\\n          className=\\"flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-slate-400 bg-white text-slate-700 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40\\"\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          <ChevronRight size={18} />\\r\\n        </button>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 27.1 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"009cf4","wall_time_seconds":0.8547814,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":2277,"output":"/**\\r\\n * The RC drill session state machine.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Holds every passage\'s questions as one continuously numbered list, tracks what\\r\\n * the student has selected, crossed out, flagged and had revealed, and decides\\r\\n * when an attempt is written to the database. The three reveal modes differ only\\r\\n * in *when* the reveal happens; the shape of the state is the same for all.\\r\\n *\\r\\n *   unanswered -> selected -> (confirmed) -> revealed\\r\\n */\\r\\nimport type {\\r\\n  RcDrillConfig,\\r\\n  RcLetter,\\r\\n  RcPassage,\\r\\n  RcQuestion,\\r\\n  RcRevealMode,\\r\\n} from \'./rcTypes\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/** One question, flattened out of its passage, with its session-wide number. */\\r\\nexport interface RcSessionItem {\\r\\n  question: RcQuestion;\\r\\n  passage: RcPassage;\\r\\n  /** 1-based across the whole session, not restarted per passage. */\\r\\n  number: number;\\r\\n  passageIndex: number;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface RcSessionState {\\r\\n  items: RcSessionItem[];\\r\\n  currentIndex: number;\\r\\n  answers: Map<number, RcLetter>;\\r\\n  eliminations: Map<number, RcLetter[]>;\\r\\n  flagged: Set<number>;\\r\\n  /** Questions whose feedback is showing. */\\r\\n  revealed: Set<number>;\\r\\n  /** Questions already written to rc_attempts in this session. */\\r\\n  recorded: Set<number>;\\r\\n  /** Passages switched into review state (per_passage mode). */\\r\\n  reviewedPassages: Set<number>;\\r\\n  paused: boolean;\\r\\n  submitted: boolean;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function buildItems(passages: RcPassage[]): RcSessionItem[] {\\r\\n  const items: RcSessionItem[] = [];\\r\\n  let number = 1;\\r\\n  passages.forEach((passage, passageIndex) => {\\r\\n    for (const question of passage.questions) {\\r\\n      items.push({ question, passage, number: number++, passageIndex });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  return items;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function initialState(passages: RcPassage[]): RcSessionState {\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    items: buildItems(passages),\\r\\n    currentIndex: 0,\\r\\n    answers: new Map(),\\r\\n    eliminations: new Map(),\\r\\n    flagged: new Set(),\\r\\n    revealed: new Set(),\\r\\n    recorded: new Set(),\\r\\n    reviewedPassages: new Set(),\\r\\n    paused: false,\\r\\n    submitted: false,\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ queries */\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const currentItem = (s: RcSessionState): RcSessionItem | undefined => s.items[s.currentIndex];\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function itemsForPassage(s: RcSessionState, passageId: number): RcSessionItem[] {\\r\\n  return s.items.filter((i) => i.passage.passageId === passageId);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Distinct passages in session order. */\\r\\nexport function passageOrder(s: RcSessionState): RcPassage[] {\\r\\n  const seen = new Map<number, RcPassage>();\\r\\n  for (const i of s.items) if (!seen.has(i.passage.passageId)) seen.set(i.passage.passageId, i.passage);\\r\\n  return [...seen.values()];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const isAnswered = (s: RcSessionState, questionId: number) => s.answers.has(questionId);\\r\\n\\r\\n/** True once every question of the passage carries a selection. */\\r\\nexport function passageComplete(s: RcSessionState, passageId: number): boolean {\\r\\n  const items = itemsForPassage(s, passageId);\\r\\n  return items.length > 0 && items.every((i) => s.answers.has(i.question.questionId));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function unansweredCount(s: RcSessionState): number {\\r\\n  return s.items.filter((i) => !s.answers.has(i.question.questionId)).length;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function isCorrect(item: RcSessionItem, selected: RcLetter | null | undefined): boolean | null {\\r\\n  if (!selected) return null;\\r\\n  return selected === item.question.correctAnswer;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Whether feedback should be visible for a question right now.\\r\\n * per_question reveals one at a time, per_passage a passage at a time, and\\r\\n * end_of_set nothing at all until the session is submitted.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function isRevealed(s: RcSessionState, mode: RcRevealMode, item: RcSessionItem): boolean {\\r\\n  if (s.submitted) return true;\\r\\n  switch (mode) {\\r\\n    case \'per_question\':\\r\\n      return s.revealed.has(item.question.questionId);\\r\\n    case \'per_passage\':\\r\\n      return s.reviewedPassages.has(item.passage.passageId);\\r\\n    case \'end_of_set\':\\r\\n      return false;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The pager row: every question in the session gets its own pill, with a\\r\\n * divider between passages — the official interface shows all of them at once\\r\\n * (1-7 | 8-15 | 16-21 | 22-27) rather than collapsing the passages you are not\\r\\n * currently in, so a student can see the whole section\'s shape and jump\\r\\n * anywhere in it.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport interface RcPagerEntry {\\r\\n  kind: \'question\' | \'divider\';\\r\\n  passageId: number;\\r\\n  /** Index into items. -1 for a divider, which is not navigable. */\\r\\n  index: number;\\r\\n  label: string;\\r\\n  questionId?: number;\\r\\n  sourceLabel?: string;\\r\\n  passageLabel?: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function pagerEntries(s: RcSessionState): RcPagerEntry[] {\\r\\n  const entries: RcPagerEntry[] = [];\\r\\n\\r\\n  for (const passage of passageOrder(s)) {\\r\\n    const items = itemsForPassage(s, passage.passageId);\\r\\n    if (items.length === 0) continue;\\r\\n\\r\\n    if (entries.length > 0) {\\r\\n      entries.push({\\r\\n        kind: \'divider\',\\r\\n        passageId: passage.passageId,\\r\\n        index: -1,\\r\\n        label: \'\',\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    for (const item of items) {\\r\\n      entries.push({\\r\\n        kind: \'question\',\\r\\n        passageId: passage.passageId,\\r\\n        index: s.items.indexOf(item),\\r\\n        label: String(item.number),\\r\\n        questionId: item.question.questionId,\\r\\n        sourceLabel: `PT ${passage.preptest} · Section ${passage.section} · Passage ${passage.setNumber} · Question ${item.question.position}`,\\r\\n        passageLabel: `PT ${passage.preptest} · Section ${passage.section} · Passage ${passage.setNumber}${passage.title ? ` · ${passage.title}` : \'\'}`,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return entries;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- reduction */\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type RcSessionAction =\\r\\n  | { type: \'goto\'; index: number }\\r\\n  | { type: \'next\' }\\r\\n  | { type: \'prev\' }\\r\\n  | { type: \'select\'; questionId: number; letter: RcLetter }\\r\\n  | { type: \'toggleElimination\'; questionId: number; letter: RcLetter }\\r\\n  | { type: \'toggleFlag\'; questionId: number }\\r\\n  | { type: \'reveal\'; questionId: number }\\r\\n  | { type: \'reviewPassage\'; passageId: number }\\r\\n  | { type: \'markRecorded\'; questionIds: number[] }\\r\\n  | { type: \'setPaused\'; paused: boolean }\\r\\n  | { type: \'submit\' }\\r\\n  | { type: \'restore\'; state: Partial<RcSessionState> };\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function rcSessionReducer(state: RcSessionState, action: RcSessionAction): RcSessionState {\\r\\n  switch (action.type) {\\r\\n    case \'goto\':\\r\\n      return { ...state, currentIndex: Math.max(0, Math.min(action.index, state.items.length - 1)) };\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'next\':\\r\\n      return { ...state, currentIndex: Math.min(state.currentIndex + 1, state.items.length - 1) };\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'prev\':\\r\\n      return { ...state, currentIndex: Math.max(state.currentIndex - 1, 0) };\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'select\': {\\r\\n      // Selecting a crossed-out choice un-crosses it.\\r\\n      const eliminations = new Map(state.eliminations);\\r\\n      const current = eliminations.get(action.questionId) ?? [];\\r\\n      if (current.includes(action.letter)) {\\r\\n        eliminations.set(\\r\\n          action.questionId,\\r\\n          current.filter((l) => l !== action.letter),\\r\\n        );\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      const answers = new Map(state.answers);\\r\\n      answers.set(action.questionId, action.letter);\\r\\n      return { ...state, answers, eliminations };\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'toggleElimination\': {\\r\\n      const eliminations = new Map(state.eliminations);\\r\\n      const current = eliminations.get(action.questionId) ?? [];\\r\\n      eliminations.set(\\r\\n        action.questionId,\\r\\n        current.includes(action.letter)\\r\\n          ? current.filter((l) => l !== action.letter)\\r\\n          : [...current, action.letter],\\r\\n      );\\r\\n      return { ...state, eliminations };\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'toggleFlag\': {\\r\\n      const flagged = new Set(state.flagged);\\r\\n      if (flagged.has(action.questionId)) flagged.delete(action.questionId);\\r\\n      else flagged.add(action.questionId);\\r\\n      return { ...state, flagged };\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'reveal\': {\\r\\n      const revealed = new Set(state.revealed);\\r\\n      revealed.add(action.questionId);\\r\\n      return { ...state, revealed };\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'reviewPassage\': {\\r\\n      const reviewedPassages = new Set(state.reviewedPassages);\\r\\n      reviewedPassages.add(action.passageId);\\r\\n      return { ...state, reviewedPassages };\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'markRecorded\': {\\r\\n      const recorded = new Set(state.recorded);\\r\\n      for (const id of action.questionIds) recorded.add(id);\\r\\n      return { ...state, recorded };\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'setPaused\':\\r\\n      return { ...state, paused: action.paused };\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'submit\':\\r\\n      return { ...state, submitted: true, paused: false };\\r\\n\\r\\n    case \'restore\':\\r\\n      return { ...state, ...action.state };\\r\\n\\r\\n    default:\\r\\n      return state;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Default per-passage budget when a session is timed: 8:45. */\\r\\nexport const DEFAULT_SECONDS_PER_PASSAGE = 525;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function totalBudgetSeconds(config: RcDrillConfig, passageCount: number): number | null {\\r\\n  if (config.timing.mode !== \'per_passage_budget\') return null;\\r\\n  return config.timing.secondsPerPassage * passageCount;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script running with cell ID 42\nWall time 1.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"f3194b","wall_time_seconds":1.6103186,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1691,"output":"import * as React from \'react\';\\r\\nimport { setAttemptFlag } from \'../api/rcProgress\';\\r\\nimport type { RcAnnotationSaveStatus } from \'./useRcHighlights\';\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface Options {\\r\\n  actorId?: string | null;\\r\\n  sessionId?: string | null;\\r\\n  enabled: boolean;\\r\\n  recordedQuestionIds: number[];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface Intent { flagged: boolean; version: number }\\r\\ninterface Owner {\\r\\n  actor: string; session: string; cancelled: boolean;\\r\\n  pending: Map<number, Intent>; saving: Map<number, number>; chains: Map<number, Promise<void>>;\\r\\n}\\r\\nconst prefixFor = (actor: string, session: string) => `rc:flags:pending:${encodeURIComponent(actor)}:${encodeURIComponent(session)}:`;\\r\\nconst keyFor = (owner: Owner, questionId: number) => `${prefixFor(owner.actor, owner.session)}${questionId}`;\\r\\nconst EMPTY_FLAGS = new Map<number, boolean>();\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Personal drill flags only. The player owns answer creation and source restrictions. */\\r\\nexport function useRcFlagPersistence({ actorId, sessionId, enabled, recordedQuestionIds }: Options) {\\r\\n  const context = React.useRef<Owner | null>(null);\\r\\n  const version = React.useRef(0);\\r\\n  const recorded = React.useRef(recordedQuestionIds);\\r\\n  recorded.current = recordedQuestionIds;\\r\\n  const [pendingFlags, setPendingFlags] = React.useState(new Map<number, boolean>());\\r\\n  const [statuses, setStatuses] = React.useState(new Map<number, RcAnnotationSaveStatus>());\\r\\n  const [errors, setErrors] = React.useState(new Map<number, string>());\\r\\n  const [recoveryError, setRecoveryError] = React.useState<string | null>(null);\\r\\n  const matches = (owner: Owner | null) => !!owner && !owner.cancelled && enabled && owner.actor === actorId && owner.session === sessionId;\\r\\n\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    const owner: Owner = { actor: actorId ?? \'\', session: sessionId ?? \'\', cancelled: false, pending: new Map(), saving: new Map(), chains: new Map() };\\r\\n    context.current = owner;\\r\\n    const flags = new Map<number, boolean>();\\r\\n    const recovered = new Map<number, RcAnnotationSaveStatus>();\\r\\n    setErrors(new Map());\\r\\n    setRecoveryError(null);\\r\\n    if (actorId && sessionId && enabled) {\\r\\n      try {\\r\\n        const prefix = prefixFor(actorId, sessionId);\\r\\n        for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {\\r\\n          const key = localStorage.key(i);\\r\\n          if (!key?.startsWith(prefix)) continue;\\r\\n          const questionId = Number(key.slice(prefix.length));\\r\\n          const value = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) ?? \'null\') as { flagged: boolean } | null;\\r\\n          if (!Number.isSafeInteger(questionId) || questionId < 1 || typeof value?.flagged !== \'boolean\') throw new Error(\'Pending flags could not be read.\');\\r\\n          owner.pending.set(questionId, { flagged: value.flagged, version: ++version.current });\\r\\n          flags.set(questionId, value.flagged);\\r\\n          recovered.set(questionId, \'recovered\');\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      } catch (error: unknown) {\\r\\n        setRecoveryError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : \'Pending flags could not be read.\');\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    setPendingFlags(flags);\\r\\n    setStatuses(recovered);\\r\\n    return () => { owner.cancelled = true; };\\r\\n  }, [actorId, sessionId, enabled]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  function acknowledge(owner: Owner, questionId: number, intent: Intent) {\\r\\n    const raw = localStorage.getItem(keyFor(owner, questionId));\\r\\n    if (raw && JSON.parse(raw).flagged === intent.flagged && owner.pending.get(questionId)?.version === intent.version) localStorage.removeItem(keyFor(owner, questionId));\\r\\n    if (matches(owner) && owner.pending.get(questionId)?.version === intent.version) {\\r\\n      owner.pending.delete(questionId);\\r\\n      setPendingFlags((map) => { const next = new Map(map); next.delete(questionId); return next; });\\r\\n      setStatuses((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, \'saved\'));\\r\\n      setErrors((map) => { const next = new Map(map); next.delete(questionId); return next; });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  function retry(questionId: number, justRecorded = false) {\\r\\n    const owner = context.current;\\r\\n    if (!matches(owner) || !owner) return;\\r\\n    const intent = owner.pending.get(questionId);\\r\\n    if (!intent || owner.saving.get(questionId) === intent.version) return;\\r\\n    if (!justRecorded && !recorded.current.includes(questionId)) return;\\r\\n    owner.saving.set(questionId, intent.version);\\r\\n    setStatuses((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, \'saving\'));\\r\\n    const previous = owner.chains.get(questionId) ?? Promise.resolve();\\r\\n    const task = previous.catch(() => undefined).then(async () => {\\r\\n      if (owner.cancelled) return;\\r\\n      try {\\r\\n        await setAttemptFlag(owner.session, questionId, intent.flagged, owner.actor);\\r\\n        acknowledge(owner, questionId, intent);\\r\\n      } catch (error: unknown) {\\r\\n        if (matches(owner) && owner.pending.get(questionId)?.version === intent.version) {\\r\\n          setStatuses((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, \'error\'));\\r\\n          setErrors((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, error instanceof Error ? error.message : \'The flag was not saved.\'));\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      } finally {\\r\\n        if (owner.saving.get(questionId) === intent.version) owner.saving.delete(questionId);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    owner.chains.set(questionId, task);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  function setFlag(questionId: number, flagged: boolean) {\\r\\n    const owner = context.current;\\r\\n    if (!matches(owner) || !owner) return;\\r\\n    const intent = { flagged, version: ++version.current };\\r\\n    owner.pending.set(questionId, intent);\\r\\n    setPendingFlags((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, flagged));\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      localStorage.setItem(keyFor(owner, questionId), JSON.stringify({ flagged }));\\r\\n      setStatuses((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, \'recovered\'));\\r\\n    } catch {\\r\\n      setStatuses((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, \'error\'));\\r\\n      setErrors((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, \'The device could not keep a recovery copy. Retry saving before leaving.\'));\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    retry(questionId);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  function acknowledgeRecordedFlag(questionId: number, flagged: boolean) {\\r\\n    const owner = context.current;\\r\\n    if (!matches(owner) || !owner) return;\\r\\n    const intent = owner.pending.get(questionId);\\r\\n    if (intent?.flagged === flagged) {\\r\\n      try { acknowledge(owner, questionId, intent); }\\r\\n      catch { setStatuses((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, \'error\')); }\\r\\n    } else if (intent) retry(questionId, true);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const valid = matches(context.current);\\r\\n  return { pendingFlags: valid ? pendingFlags : EMPTY_FLAGS, setFlag, retry, acknowledgeRecordedFlag,\\r\\n    recoveryError: valid ? recoveryError : null,\\r\\n    statusForQuestion: (id: number): RcAnnotationSaveStatus => valid ? statuses.get(id) ?? \'idle\' : \'idle\',\\r\\n    errorForQuestion: (id: number) => valid ? errors.get(id) ?? null : null,\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"4bdd7a","wall_time_seconds":1.4952857,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":396,"output":"  // answered cross-outs travel on the attempt row. This adds bookmarks, and\\r\\n  // keeps an unanswered question\'s cross-outs on this device for this\\r\\n  // session so a reload does not lose them. A timed or practice-test\\r\\n  // section, and its protected review, get none of it.\\r\\n  const studyAidsEnabled =\\r\\n    !!user?.id && !config.timedSectionSittingId && !config.practiceTestRunId && !protectedReview;\\r\\n  const studyTools = useQuestionStudyTools(studyAidsEnabled ? user?.id : null);\\r\\n  /** Session-scoped, so a later drill of the same question starts clean. */\\r\\n  const rcCrossOutRef = (sid: string, questionId: number) => ({ family: \'rc\' as const, id: `${sid}:${questionId}` });\\r\\n  const changedCrossOuts = React.useRef(new Set<number>());\\r\\n  const flagPersistence = useRcFlagPersistence({\\r\\n    actorId: user?.id, sessionId,\\r\\n    enabled: !!sessionId && sessionOwnerId === user?.id && !loadingAnswers && !answerLoadError && !protectedReview,\\r\\n    recordedQuestionIds: [...state.recorded],\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    for (const [questionId, flagged] of flagPersistence.pendingFlags) {\\r\\n      if (state.flagged.has(questionId) !== flagged) dispatch({ type: \'toggleFlag\', questionId });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }, [flagPersistence.pendingFlags, state.flagged]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (!changedCrossOuts.current.size || loadingAnswers || answerLoadError || !studyAidsEnabled || reviewOnly || !sessionId || sessionOwnerId !== user?.id || sessionStatus !== \'in_progress\') return;\\r\\n    const changed = [...changedCrossOuts.current];\\r\\n    changedCrossOuts.current.clear();\\r\\n"}'}]

{ label: \'Finish drill\', disabled: false, onClick: handleSubmit }\\r\\n        : {\\r\\n            label: \'Continue to next passage\',\\r\\n            disabled: false,\\r\\n            onClick: () => goTo(nextPassageItem),\\r\\n          };\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    return { label: \'Submit\', disabled: false, onClick: () => setModal(\'submit\') };\\r\\n  })();\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"fa5b51","wall_time_seconds":1.5709075000000001,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":168,"output":"  const questionStart = React.useRef<number>(Date.now());\\r\\n\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (state.paused || loadingAnswers || answerLoadError || !sessionId || !passages || reviewOnly) return;\\r\\n    let lastTick = Date.now();\\r\\n    const id = window.setInterval(() => {\\r\\n      const now = Date.now();\\r\\n      const delta = Math.max(0, (now - lastTick) / 1000);\\r\\n      lastTick = now;\\r\\n      setElapsed((e) => {\\r\\n        const next = e + delta;\\r\\n        writeClockBackup(sessionId, next);\\r\\n        return next;\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }, 1000);\\r\\n    return () => window.clearInterval(id);\\r\\n  }, [state.paused, loadingAnswers, answerLoadError, sessionId, passages, reviewOnly]);\\r\\n"}'}]

setFontScale(value); }}\\r\\n        fontScaleOptions={RC_FONT_SCALE_OPTIONS} />;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    render(<Toolbar />);\\r\\n    const trigger = screen.getByRole(\'button\', { name: \'Text size\' });\\r\\n    fireEvent.click(trigger);\\r\\n    const preferences = screen.getByRole(\'dialog\', { name: \'Reading preferences\' });\\r\\n    expect(within(preferences).getByRole(\'button\', { name: \'Default (100%)\' })).toHaveAttribute(\'aria-pressed\', \'true\');\\r\\n    fireEvent.click(within(preferences).getByRole(\'button\', { name: \'Large (125%)\' }));\\r\\n    expect(selectedSize).toHaveBeenCalledWith(1.25);\\r\\n    expect(screen.queryByRole(\'dialog\', { name: \'Reading preferences\' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();\\r\\n    expect(trigger).toHaveAttribute(\'title\', \'Text size: Large (125%)\');\\r\\n    await waitFor(() => expect(trigger).toHaveFocus());\\r\\n    fireEvent.click(trigger);\\r\\n    expect(screen.getByRole(\'button\', { name: \'Large (125%)\' })).toHaveAttribute(\'aria-pressed\', \'true\');\\r\\n    expect(screen.getByText(\'Current size: Large (125%)\')).toBeInTheDocument();\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\'preserves the shared LR default font choices\', () => {\\r\\n    render(<OfficialDrillToolbar highlightMode=\\"none\\" setHighlightMode={vi.fn()} timerLabel=\\"\\"\\r\\n      searchTerm=\\"\\" setSearchTerm={vi.fn()} fontScale={1} setFontScale={vi.fn()} />);\\r\\n    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole(\'button\', { name: \'Text size\' }));\\r\\n    expect(screen.getByRole(\'button\', { name: \'Default\' })).toHaveAttribute(\'aria-pressed\', \'true\');\\r\\n    expect(screen.getByRole(\'button\', { name: \'Extra Large\' })).toBeInTheDocument();\\r\\n  });\\r\\n});\\r\\n"}'}]
